h1. PokéAPI

All the Pokémon data you'll ever need in one place, easily accessible through a modern free open-source RESTful API.

## What is this?

This is a full RESTful API linked to an extensive database detailing everything about the Pokémon main game series.

We've covered everything from Pokémon to Berry Flavors.

## Where do I start?

We have awesome [documentation\](https://pokeapi.co/docs/v2) on how to use this API. It takes minutes to get started.

This API will always be publicly available and will never require any extensive setup process to consume.

Created by [**Paul Hallett**\](https://github.com/phalt) and other [**PokéAPI contributors**\](https://github.com/PokeAPI/pokeapi#contributing) around the world. Pokémon and Pokémon character names are trademarks of Nintendo.

*Version:* 2.10.0

----

h2. Endpoints


h3. berryFirmnessList
{panel:title=berryFirmnessList|borderStyle=solid|borderColor=#003b6f|titleBGColor=#003b6f|titleColor=#a6b8c7|bgColor=#ffffff}
    *Summary:* List berry firmness
    *Description:* Berries can be soft or hard. Check out [Bulbapedia\](http://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Category:Berries_by_firmness) for greater detail.

	|| HttpMethod | {status:colour=Yellow|title=get|subtle=false} |
    || Protocol | {noformat:nopanel=true}https{noformat} |
    || Path | {noformat:nopanel=true}/api/v2/berry-firmness/{noformat} |
    || Base Path | {noformat:nopanel=true}https://pokeapi.co{noformat} |
    || Produces | {noformat:nopanel=true}application/json {noformat} |
----
h4. Parameters



h5. Query Parameters
        ||Name||Description||Type||Required||Default||Pattern||Enum||Example||
        |limit |Number of results to return per page. | Integer | (x) |null |  |  ||offset |The initial index from which to return the results. | Integer | (x) |null |  |  ||q |&gt; Only available locally and not at [pokeapi.co\](https://pokeapi.co/docs/v2)
Case-insensitive query applied on the &#x60;name&#x60; property.  | String | (x) |null |  |  |
----

dependency and compilation order fight eachother and I'm
